# Hoverline sample library: credits

Every recording in `samples/` is CC0 1.0 (public domain) or CC BY 4.0, or was synthesised for Hoverline (CC0). Nothing here is BY-SA, NC, "royalty-free" or of unverified origin.

## Sources

Links point at the exact commits the files were taken from.

| Source | Licence | Credit line | Where |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Sonic Pi sample pack (freesound.org recordings) | CC0 1.0 | Drum hits from the Sonic Pi sample pack (freesound.org uploads by menegass, looppool and others; CC0), as lossless FLAC originals | https://github.com/sonic-pi-net/sonic-pi/tree/main/etc/samples |
| Virtuosity Drums (Versilian Studios with Karoryfer Samples) | CC0 1.0 | Acoustic drums and auxiliary percussion: Virtuosity Drums by Versilian Studios and Karoryfer Samples, played by Austin McMahon (CC0) | https://github.com/sfzinstruments/virtuosity_drums |
| Versilian Community Sample Library | CC0 1.0 | Percussion, timpani, concert bass drum, cymbals and didgeridoo: the Versilian Community Sample Library (CC0) | https://github.com/sgossner/VCSL |
| VSCO-2 Community Edition | CC0 1.0 | Orchestral percussion: VSCO-2 Community Edition by Versilian Studios (CC0) | https://github.com/sgossner/VSCO-2-CE |
| MuldjordKit (FreePats version) by Lars Muldjord | CC BY 4.0 | Rock and metal kit: MuldjordKit by Lars Muldjord (FreePats version by Roberto), CC BY 4.0. Drum samples provided by DrumGizmo.org. | https://github.com/freepats/muldjordkit |
| FreePats Hang tuned in D minor | CC0 1.0 | Hang drum: FreePats "Hang tuned in D minor", played by Mar, recorded by Gonzalo and Roberto (CC0) | https://github.com/freepats/hang-D-minor |
| FreePats Electric Guitar FSBS | CC0 1.0 | Electric guitar: FreePats Electric Guitar FSBS (direct, clean, dist1 and dist2 banks, CC0) | https://github.com/freepats/electric-guitar-FSBS-dist1 |
| FreePats Finger Bass YR / Picked Bass YR | CC0 1.0 | Electric bass: FreePats Finger Bass YR and Picked Bass YR by Andrea Biasior (CC0) | https://github.com/freepats/electric-bass-YR |
| FreePats World percussion | CC0 1.0 | Cajon, bongos, shakers, tambourine, castanets, maracas and darbuka: FreePats World percussion, played by Xavimart (CC0) | https://github.com/freepats/world-percussion |
| Synthesised for Hoverline | CC0 1.0 | Electronic drums (808, 909, techno, house, trance, psytrance, hardcore, drum & bass): synthesised offline for Hoverline | https://github.com/lucidfir-ops/HoverLine |

CC BY 4.0 (MuldjordKit) requires this credit wherever the game or its music is published: "Drum samples provided by DrumGizmo.org." (MuldjordKit by Lars Muldjord, FreePats version by Roberto).

## Every recording

Sound id, its source and licence, and the original files it was made from (several when mic positions were mixed; "derived" round robins are small retunings of the recording before them).
